## 19 November 2026 EAT — private Cloud Run invocation, checkpoint 454

Implemented explicit server-only workload invocation for the web customer and admin branch BFFs. The fixed canonical Cloud Run audience requires attached staging workload settings. Google auth library 11.3.4 obtains/caches its service token lazily; application fetch keeps the independent Firebase user token in Authorization and puts workload identity only in X-Serverless-Authorization. A run.app target without the exact configuration refuses. Browser BFFs reject caller-supplied service credentials. The API continues to verify Firebase UID binding and current SQL membership; service headers supply no actor/company authority.

The transport shares in-flight token acquisition, bounds each caller's wait, propagates cancellation, refuses redirects/alternate origins/cookies/actor headers and sends each application request once. Failed identity acquisition cannot fall back to unauthenticated fetch. The Google client never sends application traffic or retries a mutation. ## 19 November 2026 EAT — owned staging API runtime, checkpoint 452

Added a separate start:hosted API entry point. It requires NODE_ENV=production, APP_ENV=staging, matching GOOGLE_CLOUD_PROJECT and an exact realtor-staging-api-v1 configuration with fixed company/HTTPS origin/privacy version. Preview variables, API_MODE, emulator settings, credential-file overrides, disabled TLS and unsafe database URL parameters refuse. Startup attests the dedicated database login before listening; the existing transaction adapter repeats role safety checks per transaction. It never installs migrations, seeds data, enrolls Firebase users or falls back to owner/demo credentials. The hosted handler exposes only current customer account/favourites/original-operation and staff session/branch protocols. The shared customer core has a distinct hosted constructor with no preview extensions; the old customer candidate and original development HTTP entry still refuse production. A new explicit branch-only service mode leaves invitations, memberships and unrelated staff modes disabled. The outer whitelist rejects every other route before token verification or SQL. No generic proxy or normal production/cutover profile was added.

The new firebase-api-binding-lookup.sql proposal grants the API runtime only a security-definer lookup function, scoped to the fixed company and current active membership. It reads active project/auth-tenant/UID mappings without granting the API role direct SELECT on identity tables, browser-state writes, enrollment rights or new membership. Firebase signature/revocation/email policy and active UID binding still precede current SQL resource authorization. The ordinary login attestation was exercised against actual disposable SQL roles; the migration owner refused and the isolated NOINHERIT api_runtime-member login passed. No real Cloud SQL login was tested.

The Node HTTP envelope fixes origin to the configured HTTPS Host, rejects ambiguous credentials/caller identities, aliases, unsupported routes/methods, cross-origin bodies and oversized/framing-invalid requests. It bounds incoming JSON and response size, sets request/header/keepalive timeouts, propagates disconnect cancellation and closes the database during shutdown. Proxy routing/trace fields never supply actor/company authority. The health route reports process liveness only; it is not proof that all schema, provider or release gates passed. HTTPS termination/ingress/probe behavior still needs Cloud Run verification. Idle database errors have a generic handler rather than uncaught credential-bearing diagnostics.

## 19 November 2026 EAT — hosted admin branch integration, checkpoint 451

A separate hosted admin branch workspace now opens at admin / and /staff when HOSTED_STAFF_API_ORIGIN is explicitly configured with Firebase identity/database settings and PRIVACY_NOTICE_VERSION. The root is dynamic, so deployment runtime configuration cannot leave the old static preview as the post-login destination. Invalid hosted setup renders unavailable and never falls through to synthetic staff authority. The new /api/hosted-staff route supports only branch session/list/current/create-edit/original/retry/settle/logout; membership changes, invitation issuance/redemption, listing drafts/reviews and other existing staff routes remain their separate preview-only paths, not promoted by this work.

The server authenticates the opaque Firebase session, binds protected API staff context to the same company/actor, then requires current SQL company-admin branch access before reading private original state and again before returning data. Posted roles/identities, preview headers, wrong origins and CSRF are refused. Branch commands reserve encrypted SQL state per company/actor before dispatch. Lost responses, server-component recreation and later same-actor login retain the original; another actor cannot read it. Only a successful original read permits settlement; a missing-original read permits one deliberate replay of the same bytes. Receipt validation binds all command fields and the original actor. Logout still works after company membership revocation. No bearer token enters client props, URLs, browser storage or original-command rows.

Customer and staff now share an allowlisted encrypted original-store implementation and bounded JSON reader. Their SQL tables/associated-data namespaces stay distinct. The customer namespace and byte format remain unchanged; a test independently constructs a pre-refactor encrypted customer row and reads it without rewriting. The staff table is a separate standalone SQL proposal, with reviewed identity-runtime grants and account-erasure/retention still required before launch.

The client screen waits for fresh authority after hydration; SSR has no private rows or mutation form. It clears private views and typed branch reasons on hidden visibility, sign-in broadcasts, denied access, logout and expiry, and refreshes on focus/back-forward restoration. A synchronous write lock plus generation/abort guards suppress late UI responses. Ambiguous outcomes block further edits until refresh/recovery; no automatic mutation retry occurs. This logic has not been accepted through actual browser hydration, cross-tab, visibility, back-forward, assistive technology or device checks.

## Verified work

The local vendor resources, UAT folder and two delivery media folders were inventoried and snapshotted. SHA-256 hashes of every archived file were verified by reading the snapshots back. Generated build/cache directories and Git internals are excluded and listed in the manifest. Original files were not edited. Read-only snapshot copies live outside this repository in ../rebuild-preservation/2026-10-09. This is a same-device recovery copy, not an independent disaster-recovery backup.

Remote Figma and Miro documents are references, not fully preserved exports. Obtain owner exports/history before declaring design preservation complete. The supplied PDF/proposal are included in the vendor snapshot. Additional media not in the listed source roots must be inventoried before migration.

## Proposed pilot scope

Responsive public website, Android/iOS application, admin portal, shared identity, property catalogue and resilient media, search, favourites, agent contact, viewing requests, moderation, lead assignment/status, simple operational reporting and configuration for a second realtor. Deliver and verify a listing-to-enquiry vertical slice before expanding. Payments, paid ads, commission settlement, AI recommendations and advanced marketplace growth are later scope. No synthetic UAT listings enter real stock.

## Architecture decisions — provisional until Stage 01 review

One TypeScript monorepo now implements apps/web (Next.js), apps/admin (Next.js), apps/mobile (Expo/React Native), apps/api (modular TypeScript service), packages/domain, packages/demo, packages/api-client and the Node-only experimental packages/media. V2 pricing/location inputs now converge across shared validators, revision storage, reviewed snapshots, API and public client; public web now deliberately connects to v2 through server-only configuration with exact disclosure; local authenticated existing-draft editing now uses raw v2 inputs and recoverable optimistic saves. The local synthetic admin review queue/detail/decisions are implemented; real sessions, full navigation/pagination, native operational integration and legacy staff contract convergence remain follow-up work. See moderation.md and staff-drafts.md. Shared business contracts and semantic brand configuration support platform-appropriate UI. Exact versions and compatibility reasons are recorded in development.md and package-lock.json. PostgreSQL with PostGIS remains the production target; current tested migrations use bounded coordinates without the extension. Object storage/media processing and providers are not provisioned.

Tenant identity is resolved and authorized on the server; every tenant resource, object-storage path, query, job and audit event is scoped. Include automated cross-tenant denial tests. Company name, logo, colors, domains, contact details, feature switches and policies are validated configuration. New client onboarding must pass a two-tenant reproduction test. Separate deployments/databases remain an option for contractual isolation.

| Asset/account | Current evidence | Required next step | Owner |

|---|---|---|---|

| New source | Local separate repository on Boniface computer | Choose Boniface-controlled private remote and independent backup | Boniface |

| Vendor source/assets/design | Files supplied; rights not verified | Review contract/licences and asset permissions; write replacement code independently | Boniface |

| Customer data | No authorized production export inspected | Approve field mapping, lawful migration and retention; exclude passwords/test fixtures | Boniface + realtor |

| Firebase/hosting | Existing URLs known; account control unverified | Inventory owner/admin access and export capability | Boniface/vendor |

| Domains/DNS/email | Unknown | Confirm registrar/admin control and intended domains | Boniface |

| Figma/Miro | Links supplied; full exports pending | Obtain owned exports and reusable brand assets | Boniface |

| Replacement providers | Not selected | Compare recurring cost, backups, region, portability and support | Engineering + Boniface |

## Repository and contribution rules

Treat new source as private pending an explicit licensing decision. No open-source licence is granted by this document. Contributors must have agreed rights to contribute. Do not copy vendor implementation into replacement code. Preserve requirements and permitted assets separately. Never commit secrets, signing keys, credentials or customer exports. Review dependency licences before adoption. Use reviewed changes and CI before production; keep schema migrations reversible where practical. Remote repository and access policies remain pending; a local Git repository alone does not establish legal ownership.

## Proposed success and release gates

The realtor can publish accurate stock, receive and assign enquiries, schedule viewings and follow up without a parallel unmanaged spreadsheet. Establish baseline and target measures with the realtor: enquiry response time, listing freshness, lead follow-up completion and weekly active staff. No numeric business claims until agreed. Release requires role/tenant/ownership denial tests, upload recovery, exact pricing, device and accessibility checks, end-to-end UAT, backup restore rehearsal, migration reconciliation and rollback rehearsal.

## Open decisions

Builder confirmed: Boniface with Codex. Total recurring service-price baseline: KES 10,000 per realtor per month; see service-budget.md. Weekly availability remains unknown. Broad approval permits the provisional implementation defaults in business-rules.md and acceptance-catalogue.md. Operator validation, permitted asset/data reuse, account/store control and reviewed policies remain open. Staff publish after moderation; visitors enquire; payments remain deferred. On 9 October Boniface explicitly requested UI/UX informed by supplied Figma/Miro/apps or better and authorized design improvements using research and judgment; see design-resource-register.md and ux-blueprint.md.

## Commercial and delivery baseline

See [service-budget.md](service-budget.md) for confirmed staffing/price, sourced candidate costs, proposed limits and solo delivery sequence. Allocations are estimates; no suppliers provisioned.

Use CONTINUATION.md for the latest implementation/test checkpoint and next actions.
